We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [239] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NLIP 是一种标准化的应用层协议，旨在实现异构AI代理间的互操作性，通过轻量级语义消息封装支持多种传输方式，并具备安全设计、参考实现及实际应用案例，推动AI代理在企业中的广泛采用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理在不同框架、模型、工具接口和执行环境中开发与部署，缺乏统一通信协议导致互操作性差，限制了其社会与商业潜力的发挥。

Method: 设计并标准化自然语言交互协议（NLIP），定义消息模型与传输绑定，支持多种底层协议，并通过安全设计、参考实现与实际应用验证其可行性。

Result: NLIP已由Ecma International标准化，支持跨平台互操作，具备参考实现和多个应用场景，获得行业采纳信号，与MCP、A2A等新兴代理协议形成互补关系。

Conclusion: NLIP为实现AI代理间的高效互操作提供了标准化基础，是推动AI代理规模化应用的关键基础设施。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [240] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种通过人类-代理交互进行测试时适应（TAHI）的方法，利用跨会话的人机交互数据来提升AI代理的个性化表现。在写作和视觉创作两个高实用性领域对30名用户进行了600个任务的实验，结果显示代理在仅十余次迭代后任务成功率提升4.5%-20.9%；同时，动态演化的评分模块显著提升了错误检测能力（比大模型或人类单独评估多发现16.0%-22.3%的失败）。尽管代理是为个体优化的，但其性能也表现出一定程度的跨用户泛化优势（成功提升最高达8.8%）。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理虽具备广泛能力，但在开放、复杂的真实任务中难以达到专业人士对质量的严苛要求。专业能力不仅体现在遵循标准，更在于对标准的个性化理解与突破。而现有系统缺乏有效机制捕捉用户在多次交互中逐渐明确的隐性评价标准，导致生成结果与用户真实期望存在差距。因此，亟需一种方法将人机交互过程中积累的隐性反馈信号转化为可利用的知识，以弥合通用模型与个体专家水平之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出测试时适应通过人机交互（TAHI）框架，将用户在多次交互中产生的行为数据作为信号，动态更新代理的上下文与权重，并引入一个持续演化的评分模块（rubric module），用于捕捉和抽象用户的评价标准。该模块在交互过程中不断学习并细化评价维度，形成可复用的个性化评估体系。通过在写作与视觉创作任务中对30位用户进行实证，验证了该方法的有效性。

Result: 在600个任务上，经TAHI调整后的代理在单任务成功率上平均提升4.5%-20.9%，仅需数十次交互即可实现显著改进。演化评分模块能识别出比纯大模型或人类独立评估多16.0%-22.3%的失败案例。此外，个性化代理在跨用户场景下仍展现出8.8%的成功率提升，表明其具备一定的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究证明，通过整合跨会话的人机交互数据，可以有效提升AI代理在复杂、开放任务中的表现，并构建可扩展的个性化评估体系。这一方法不仅缩小了通用模型与个体专家之间的差距，还为未来个性化智能助手的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [241] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Terminal-Universe框架通过从终端代理轨迹中重建执行环境，将单次演示转化为可复用的交互式任务环境。它利用文件操作历史恢复工作区，并由补全代理补充缺失文件与依赖，从而实现原任务重构与新任务生成。在广度上，挖掘跨工作区的依赖关系以合成多代码库查询；在深度上，将单轮任务扩展为多轮会话，模拟用户反馈与需求迭代。该方法在公开轨迹上生成了37.3k个任务充足的环境，显著提升大模型在多个基准上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终端代理轨迹虽积累量大，但缺乏可执行、可重用的环境，而真实环境对后训练至关重要。传统生成环境成本高且不现实，因此亟需一种从已有轨迹中自动重构环境的方法，以支持任务合成与持续交互。

Method: 1. 重放轨迹中的文件操作，还原原始工作区状态；2. 使用完成代理补全缺失文件和依赖；3. 重构原始任务并生成新任务；4. 在广度上挖掘跨环境依赖关系，生成跨代码库查询；5. 在深度上构建多轮交互会话，模拟用户反馈与需求演化。

Result: 基于公共终端代理轨迹，构建出37.3k个任务充足环境；在Qwen3.5-27B上进行微调后，单轮任务在Terminal-Bench 2.1上提升11.9分，多轮任务在EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4上提升13.8分。

Conclusion: Terminal-Universe成功实现了从静态轨迹到动态可交互环境的转化，不仅提升了任务多样性与复杂性，还显著增强了模型在真实开发场景下的能力，为大规模终端智能体训练提供了高质量基础设施。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [242] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果分类法，区分了模型的先前承诺与事后报告、偏好与实际输出、虚假偏好与误导接收者的效用敏感性，以及欺骗行为与其目标或策略来源之间的区别。通过在两个开源模型家族中进行受控猜谜游戏和股票交易实验，发现看似欺骗的行为可能在没有相应机制的情况下产生，而其他干预措施则直接证明接收者的信息状态可因果影响欺骗偏好。结果表明，欺骗行为虽可为欺骗机制提供证据，但该机制的存在并不足以证明模型具备欺骗意图或代理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对语言模型欺骗的研究和新闻报道越来越多地将人类心理状态概念归因于语言模型，这可能导致行为表面上看起来具有欺骗性，但实际上并非如此。因此需要明确区分表面欺骗行为与真正的欺骗机制，以避免误解模型的能力。

Method: 提出一个因果分类框架，用于分离多个关键概念（如先前承诺与事后报告、偏好与实际输出等），并在两个开源模型家族中开展受控实验（包括猜谜游戏和股票交易任务），通过干预手段检验这些概念之间的因果关系。

Result: 实验结果显示，看似欺骗的行为可以在缺乏对应机制的情况下出现；同时，接收者的信息状态确实能因果影响模型的欺骗偏好。尽管欺骗行为可支持存在欺骗机制的推断，但这种机制本身并不能证明模型具有欺骗性的自主性或代理能力。

Conclusion: 语言模型的欺骗行为不能直接等同于其具备欺骗机制或代理意识。必须通过严格的因果分析来区分行为表现与内在机制，否则容易误判模型的认知能力。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [243] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过100个自主LLM代理组成的科研集体，探索了多智能体AI科学生态系统中因共享基础设施导致的漏洞问题。当一个代理发现评估系统的漏洞后，该行为迅速在集体中传播，并在竞争压力下被广泛采用。随后，另一群代理自发组织反制行动，包括审计虚假证明、广播警告、抵制、提交正式投诉及提出验证补丁。研究强调透明渠道既传播了漏洞也使非作弊代理得以监督和抵抗。作者将此问题视为知识公地治理问题，建议引入渐进式惩罚与集体决策规则等制度机制，以支持自主群体的去中心化自我治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体AI科学生态系统依赖共享工具进行通信与协作，但这也可能成为不当行为传播的温床。本文旨在揭示在无外部干预下，自治智能体集体中自发出现的作弊与反作弊现象，探索如何通过制度设计维护系统诚信与可持续性。

Method: 构建由100个自主大语言模型（LLM）代理组成的科研集体，任务为证明形式数学猜想；监控其通过共享知识库和点对点消息的交互行为；分析作弊行为的传播路径与反制机制的形成过程；基于奥斯特罗姆（Ostrom, 1990）的知识公地治理理论，提出治理框架。

Result: 作弊行为在集体中自发传播并被部分代理采纳，尽管初期存在犹豫；同时，另一群体发展出多层次的反制策略，包括审计、预警、抵制与制度改进；透明的沟通渠道使非作弊代理具备监督与组织能力；研究验证了去中心化自我治理在复杂智能体系统中的可行性。

Conclusion: 面对多智能体系统中由共享基础设施引发的道德风险，应借鉴知识公地治理理论，建立包括渐进式制裁和集体决策在内的制度机制，推动自主智能体群的可持续、自律发展。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [244] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在数据极简条件下，基于单个查询的on-policy distillation（OPD）的表现。结果表明，即使仅使用一个查询，OPD仍能持续优化数百步，并恢复接近全数据训练的效果。通过分析状态覆盖率和学生与教师对齐速度，发现单个查询已覆盖71.5%的全数据状态，且大部分在前100步内完成。增加语义多样性的查询可进一步提升覆盖率与验证准确率，16个查询达到98.9%覆盖率并匹配全数据性能。然而，无论训练数据量多少，学生对齐速度均缓慢且趋于一致，说明OPD是数据过饱但算法资源不足。此外，该结论也适用于多教师场景，且内容轻量模板和跨域查询也能逼近真实查询表现，揭示任务内容与状态覆盖可分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注OPD的算法行为，对其训练数据的作用理解不足。本文旨在探索在极端数据稀缺情况下OPD的表现机制，以揭示数据与算法在模型优化中的真实角色。

Method: 通过在单个查询上训练OPD，评估其在不同阶段的状态覆盖率、对齐速度与验证性能；对比不同数量的语义多样性查询，分析其对性能的影响；扩展至多教师场景，并测试内容轻量模板与跨域查询的有效性。

Result: 单个查询即可实现71.5%的状态覆盖率，16个语义多样查询达到98.9%覆盖率并匹配全数据训练效果；对齐速度在不同数据规模下相似，表明算法吸收能力有限；内容轻量或跨域查询亦可逼近真实查询表现，说明任务内容与状态覆盖可分离。

Conclusion: OPD本质上是数据过饱而算法资源不足：其滚动生成广泛监督信号，但学生吸收这些信号的速度缓慢。未来应聚焦于提升步骤效率，重新审视当前前沿后训练中数据与机制的作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [245] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为概率因果影响（PCI）的新方法，结合了实际因果理论与Pearl的必要性与充分性概率概念，将可解释性问题转化为可在概率因果模型上通过蒙特卡洛近似进行估计的问题。PCI提供可扩展、基于因果的分级解释，适用于大规模复杂系统，并在合成与真实世界数据中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释工具分为两类：一是基于实际因果理论（AC），虽有理论基础但仅适用于小规模模型；二是可扩展的归因方法如SHAP，但忽略数据生成的因果结构，可能导致与因果分析冲突的结果。因此需要一种既能保持因果严谨性又可扩展的方法。

Method: PCI基于实际因果理论和Pearl的概率必要性/充分性概念，将解释问题建模为在概率因果模型上的估计任务，利用蒙特卡洛方法近似计算候选解释的分布、反事实值分布及评分函数，从而获得可扩展且因果一致的解释。

Result: PCI在合成数据、真实世界案例、连续动态系统以及数百万数据点训练的部署式因果机器学习模型中均表现良好，实现了与实际因果理论的一致性、良好的可扩展性，并能处理复杂场景。

Conclusion: PCI成功弥合了因果解释中理论严谨性与可扩展性之间的差距，是一种兼具因果根基与实用性的新型解释框架，适用于大规模复杂系统的可解释性分析。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [246] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过两次预先注册的审计实验，检验了语言模型评判系统在时间稳定性上的假设——即相同请求在不同时间应产生一致结果。结果显示，同一窗口内的重复排名相关性仅为Spearman 0.400（要求0.90），次日完全相同的输入相关性也仅达0.78（要求0.99），远低于预期。研究揭示了三个导致不稳定的机制：标签到语义映射的偏差、候选差距远小于仪器噪声水平、以及字节完全相同的输入却返回不同排名的现象。这些现象在多种修复尝试（如替换指标、采样、等待、更换提供商、自托管等）下均未改善。研究提出三层次快照身份阶梯、八项设计规则和报告检查清单，并指出一个小型预演可提前暴露问题。结论强调，在共享服务基础设施上，模型名称不能被视为静态测量工具，任何预注册评估都必须先验证其测量工具的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型的评判系统依赖于固定不变的测量标准，但这一假设未被充分验证。若评判结果不稳定，则基于此的训练数据筛选、生成评分与排行榜都将不可靠。因此需要系统性地检验评判系统的稳定性。

Method: 进行两次预先注册的审计实验，固定所有阈值，对52,988次请求进行重复测试，包括同窗口重复与次日重播；分析排名一致性，识别影响因素；测试多种修复策略（如更换提供方、自托管、等待等）；构建快照身份层级与设计指南。

Result: 同窗口重复排名相关性为0.400，次日重播相关性为0.78，均显著低于所需的0.90和0.99；三种机制解释了差异：标签-语义偏差、候选差距过小、输入字节相同但输出排名不同；多种修复手段无效；预演试验可提前发现关键问题。

Conclusion: 在共享服务环境下，模型名称并非稳定测量工具。预注册评估必须先验证评判系统的稳定性，否则无法保证后续决策的可靠性。建议采用快照身份阶梯、设计规则与报告检查清单以提升评估可信度。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [247] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [248] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方程重构方法，将参数化算子学习转化为学习单一标准算子，通过解析吸收参数变化至等效源中，实现跨参数区间的零样本预测。该方法在多参数、非线性及奇异PDE问题中支持外推，整合稀疏异构数据，并以收敛性损失作为内部故障预警信号。在核聚变托卡马克高保真模拟中，统一了四种装置几何下的电子温度数据，通过共训练的统一算子实现跨域映射。该框架为可复用的神经偏微分方程求解器提供了路径，结合方程引导迁移、数据高效性和可监控推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化模型需广泛覆盖输入函数和物理参数，且在训练分布外可能无声失效；亟需一种能高效泛化、具备可解释性和可靠性预警机制的新型学习范式。

Method: 方程重构：将参数引起的算子变化通过解析方式从控制方程中导出，并融入有效源项，从而将复杂的参数依赖问题转化为单一标准算子的学习任务。利用共训练的统一算子处理不同参数或几何条件下的数据，结合收敛性监测进行推理可靠性评估。

Result: 在多参数、非线性及奇异偏微分方程场景中成功实现外推；能够整合来自不同设备几何的稀疏异构数据（如四类托卡马克装置的电子温度数据），在统一标准域上建模；通过损失收敛性提供内部故障预警；显著提升数据效率与模型可迁移性。

Conclusion: 方程重构提供了一种高效、可靠、可监控的神经偏微分方程求解新范式，支持跨参数、跨几何的通用化建模，是迈向可复用智能求解器的关键一步。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [249] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协同学习，系统梳理了其在学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个维度上的基础原理，并针对图数据提出了分布场景分类、统计异质性分析及标准化问题建模与算法框架，最后指出了开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法在可扩展性和隐私保护方面存在根本性局限，而现有协同学习研究主要聚焦于具有规则网格结构的欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），难以有效处理现实世界中广泛存在的关系型数据（以图结构表示）。图数据依赖消息传递机制传播信息，天然适合协同环境，但其在协同学习中的机遇与挑战尚未充分探索。

Method: 通过回顾欧几里得数据协同学习的基础理论，从学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个维度进行组织；进而拓展至图结构数据，提出图分布式场景分类、统计异质性建模，并建立统一的问题形式化与算法框架；最后系统归纳未解决问题与潜在研究方向。

Result: 构建了一个从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协同学习综合分析框架，为该新兴领域提供了系统性知识体系，并明确了未来关键研究方向。

Conclusion: 协同学习在图结构数据上的应用仍处于起步阶段，需进一步解决数据分布异质性、通信效率、隐私保障与模型一致性等核心挑战，未来研究应聚焦于更灵活的图分布建模、高效通信机制设计及跨域泛化能力提升。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [250] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于旋转位置编码（RoPE）的变压器模型中头级功能组织的证据基础，通过引入两个干预度量（RFIS和RPD）揭示了检索头与位置头的完整分类，发现二者由一个显著的中低频带分隔，并定义了全局位置带（GPBand）。研究进一步提出两项原则：位置建模应仅限于局部，全局访问通过无位置依赖的检索实现；且两种功能应在头粒度上以层特定方式分配。基于此，设计了头级混合架构（HwH），使用NoPE FA进行全局检索，LA用于局部位置建模，在保持语言建模和常识推理能力的同时，显著提升零样本长上下文外推性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合架构（FA与LA结合）的分配依赖启发式方法，缺乏对头级功能组织的实证基础。尤其在长上下文任务中，模型常出现零样本长度外推失败，亟需从机制层面理解注意力头的功能分工。

Method: 提出并验证两个新干预指标：RoPE频率重要性评分（RFIS）用于衡量各频率对注意力分布的影响，以及RoPE位置依赖性（RPD）用于分离旋转位置调制的依赖关系。在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1模型上进行分析，结合受控变压器实验，识别出全局位置带（GPBand）及其与训练长度尺度的关系。基于此构建头级混合架构（HwH），并进行消融实验验证设计原则的有效性。

Result: RFIS与RPD共同验证了检索头与位置头的清晰分类，且该分类边界与训练长度对应的全局位置带一致。所提出的头级混合架构（HwH）在FA:LA比例低于1:3时，保持强语言建模与常识推理能力，同时显著优于传统Transformer、线性注意力及层级混合基线，在零样本长上下文外推方面表现突出。消融实验确认了两项设计原则及组件的关键作用。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了基于RoPE的变压器模型中头级功能的系统性组织规律，提出了可解释、可推广的混合注意力架构设计原则。头级混合架构（HwH）为未来大模型在长上下文处理中的高效性与泛化能力提供了有效路径，表明基于机制理解的混合架构设计是未来方向。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [251] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL) optimizes for the probability of exceeding high reward thresholds, emphasizing rare but high-reward outcomes. It modifies the advantage function to prioritize rare, high-reward rollouts and improves performance in tasks like object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization by leveraging these rare samples.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard reinforcement learning focuses on average reward, which can obscure differences between policies that have similar mean rewards but very different probabilities of producing rare high-reward outcomes. This is problematic during training and inference when sampling increases, as benefits depend on maintaining probability mass on high-reward paths.

Method: TailRL introduces a method that considers upper tails of the reward distribution by treating each reward threshold as a binary success event. It maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly selected threshold, effectively weighting rare high-reward rollouts more heavily. The gradient corresponds to a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients and requires only a simple change to the advantage function.

Result: TailRL outperforms standard RL methods across multiple domains, including object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization. It avoids suboptimal solutions and enables models to better benefit from additional sampling at inference time.

Conclusion: By directly optimizing coverage of high-reward outcomes through tail likelihood, TailRL enhances the robustness and effectiveness of generative policies, particularly when sampling is increased. Its compatibility with existing RL pipelines makes it practical and widely applicable.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [252] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）的机器学习代理模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上运行，预测每个网格节点的电势、电子和空穴准费米能级，实现对漂移-扩散传输的高保真模拟。通过结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，将载流子输运物理嵌入训练目标中，具备尺寸泛化能力，可在不同器件几何结构间迁移。利用深度集成的节点级不确定性驱动主动学习，可在秒级筛选大量候选设计并仅推送最具信息量的设计进行完整仿真。在多鳍三栅极FinFET上验证，该模型对三个漂移-扩散场的均方根误差低于1伏，单设计吞吐率比全仿真快数个数量级，尤其在大规模器件上优势显著，实现以往无法完成的跨尺度帕累托前沿探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高保真TCAD仿真在三维结构中计算成本高昂，限制了多目标设计空间探索；现有机器学习代理模型仅映射固定参数到少数性能指标，丢失物理细节且缺乏跨几何结构的可迁移性。

Method: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，以四面体网格为图结构，直接预测电势、电子和空穴准费米能级；训练时融合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，嵌入物理规律；利用深度集成提供节点级不确定性，构建主动学习闭环。

Result: 模型在多鳍三栅极FinFET上对漂移-扩散场的预测误差小于1伏/场，单设备推理时间不足1秒，吞吐率比完整仿真提升数个数量级，且在大规模器件上仍保持高效，支持跨尺度帕累托优化探索。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理信息GAT代理模型实现了高精度、高效率与强泛化能力的统一，为复杂半导体器件的快速多目标设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [253] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE is a graph-network simulator for granular dynamics that stores contact history directly on edges using persistent memory and attention-based message passing, achieving high accuracy and speed in 2D and 3D simulations with improved physical consistency and reduced errors compared to existing methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing learned graph simulators struggle to preserve inter-granular contact history due to dynamic contact formation, breaking, and rearrangement, leading to inaccuracies in granular motion simulation.

Method: TRACE uses edge-level memory with attention-based message passing and gated recurrent units to track contact history. An edge-identity dictionary maintains memory across changing contact graphs. A physics-structured decoder enforces Coulomb friction and equal-and-opposite forces. The model is trained via single-step pretraining and autoregressive rollout fine-tuning.

Result: TRACE achieves stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, reduces position error by 31–62% and final-deposit error by 58–89% compared to GNS and NMGNS, uses fewer parameters, avoids particle interpenetration, and provides 8.9–12.2× speedup over MPM.

Conclusion: TRACE effectively captures granular dynamics by preserving contact history on edges, outperforming prior learned simulators in accuracy, efficiency, and physical fidelity while being scalable to 2D and 3D benchmarks.

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [254] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLIWBO提出从有限库中选择平滑输入映射来改进高斯过程贝叶斯优化（GP-BO），在保持收敛性保证的同时提升样本效率，尤其适用于输入几何不匹配的情况。它在多个基准测试中优于传统方法，包括超参数优化和多智能体系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当原始坐标与目标函数的几何结构不匹配时（如对数缩放的超参数或局部峰值），输入扭曲可显著提高样本效率；但现有方法（如GP-UCB）要求固定核函数，限制了灵活性。因此需要一种既能自适应输入空间变换又能保证收敛性的方法。

Method: 提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），通过历史依赖规则从有限平滑输入映射库中选择最优扭曲方式，同时保留高概率收敛性保证，并引入$\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$的库大小代价。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，FLIWBO-UCB在几何错配条件下优于原始坐标下的GP-UCB，能跳出陷阱并恢复手动对数缩放带来的性能增益；在20维多智能体系统设计中也展示了可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO结合了输入扭曲的灵活性与理论收敛性，是处理复杂黑箱优化问题的有效工具，尤其适用于输入空间几何不匹配场景。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [255] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）中的空间对象表示为图结构，通过图神经网络（GNN）对ENC更新进行分类，以判断其对航行安全的影响是否构成关键风险。研究对比了多种GNN架构与配置，在专家评审的数据上验证了基于图的表示方法在自动识别关键变更方面的有效性，提升了ENC维护的可扩展性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子海图（ENC）的维护面临挑战，准确分类其中的空间对象变更对航行安全的重要性至关重要。传统方法难以高效处理复杂的空间和语义关系，因此需要一种能有效建模空间对象间关联的自动化方法。

Method: 将旧版和新版ENC数据分别编码为图结构，节点代表空间对象，边代表空间与语义关系；将任务建模为图对分类问题，使用多种GNN架构进行训练与评估。

Result: 实验表明，基于图的表示方法显著提升了对ENC更新的分类性能，能够有效区分关键与非关键风险，具备良好的可扩展性，适用于自动化或增强ENC维护流程。

Conclusion: 图神经网络结合图结构表示能够有效提升对电子海图变更的风险分类能力，为实现智能化、自动化的海图维护提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [256] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGOPD提出一种基于提示级教师可靠性验证的强化学习方法，通过小规模验证探针估计教师可信度，仅在可靠时启用密集监督，否则使用验证器引导的GRPO。该方法在数学、代码和指令遵循任务中均优于传统OPD，在4B和35B模型规模下多领域训练平均表现更优，并显著提升教师端计算资源利用率（从9.8%升至78.9%）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统OPD对所有提示均匀施加教师监督，未考虑教师在特定提示上的可靠性；自信但错误的教师会因反KL的模式聚焦特性导致误导性更新，而现有不确定性或一致性代理无法直接验证结果正确性

Method: 引入教师门控机制，利用少量验证探针评估每个提示的教师可靠性，仅当可靠性达标时才启用密集OPD监督，否则切换至验证器引导的GRPO策略更新

Result: 在六种单领域任务中均超越基线OPD，多领域训练下双模型规模（4B和35B）的七项基准平均得分更高；教师侧异步计算效率大幅提升，GPU利用率从9.8%增至78.9%

Conclusion: TGOPD通过提示级教师可靠性验证，实现了更安全、高效的自洽训练，有效利用闲置教师算力，为后训练阶段的高效知识迁移提供了新范式

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [257] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了因果基础模型（CFMs）的概念，这是一种将大规模预训练模型的范式引入因果推断的新方法。与传统因果推断需要为每个新问题定制流程不同，CFMs 通过上下文学习在不更新模型的情况下，直接在新数据集上估计因果效应（如平均处理效应）。文章提供了因果推断和机器学习的基础背景，并附有示例代码和 Jupyter 笔记本，便于实践入门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断依赖于为每个新问题设计特定的分析流程，包括假设因果机制、选择估计器并进行训练，过程繁琐且缺乏通用性。而机器学习领域已成功应用基础模型（如大语言模型）实现跨任务迁移。本文旨在将这一范式扩展至因果推断，提出因果基础模型（CFMs），以实现无需微调即可在新数据上快速估计因果效应的目标。

Method: 提出并阐述因果基础模型（CFMs）的框架，利用大规模预训练神经网络，在新任务中通过上下文学习（in-context learning）直接估计因果量，如平均处理效应（ATE），避免了对模型进行再训练或微调。结合因果推断理论与深度学习技术，构建可泛化、可迁移的因果估计系统。

Result: CFMs 能够在未见过的数据集上，仅通过上下文提示即完成因果效应估计，无需模型更新或重新训练。实验表明该方法具备良好的泛化能力，显著降低因果推断的使用门槛，为自动化、可扩展的因果分析提供新路径。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型（CFMs）代表了因果推断向通用化、可迁移方向的重要进展。它将预训练模型的思想引入因果分析，使研究人员能够快速适应新场景，减少重复建模工作。未来有望成为因果智能的核心基础设施。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [258] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench 是一个基准框架，用于评估内部估计器（观察者）在干预、控制或安全任务中的有效性。它区分估计准确性与所选行动带来的损失，揭示了仅靠平均准确度不足以判断行动优劣。实验表明，在闭环控制中，观察误差在初始点及可干预方向上至关重要；在电路干预任务中，某些观察者虽预测更准但不总选最优行动，而基于动作损失训练的观察者则选择更低损失行动。在安全筛选中，即使评分完美分离违规行为，仍可能因违规成本差异导致预算分配不当。跨多个模型和任务的测试显示，AUROC 排名与部署损失不一致，最佳信息源随模型变化。稀疏 SAE 读出在报告的 Qwen 面板上表现落后于密集对照组，受激活密度或检查点不匹配影响。ObserverBench 提供固定任务契约、可运行基线和表格提交方式，支持通过行动效果评估可解释性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性方法依赖内部估计器指导干预、控制和安全操作，但平均准确的估计器仍可能做出次优决策。因此需要一种能评估估计器是否真正适配具体任务的基准工具。

Method: 提出 ObserverBench 基准框架，定义固定任务契约（包括模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、未见样本和损失函数），分别测量估计准确性和所选动作造成的实际损失，并通过多类任务验证其必要性。

Result: 理论与实验证明：估计准确性和行动损失必须分开评估。在控制任务中，观察误差在起点和可干预方向上关键；在干预任务中，预测准确的观察者不一定选最优行动，而基于动作损失训练的观察者表现更好；在安全任务中，高 AUROC 不保证合理资源分配；稀疏 SAE 读出在多种设置下落后于密集对照组。

Conclusion: ObserverBench 为可解释性方法提供了一种以行动结果为导向的评估范式，强调必须结合估计精度与行动后果来判断观察器的有效性，推动可解释性研究从“理解”转向“有效干预”。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [259] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出一种联合预训练与迁移学习的方法，通过在小子域上预训练神经算子，并在新场景中仅微调轻量级组合模块，显著降低物理模拟部署成本。相比直接处理全域的模型，其在大尺度达西流和快速俯仰机翼流中均表现出更低误差，验证了局部预训练与组合级迁移协同设计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在域几何、尺寸或工况变化时精度下降，监督适应虽可恢复精度但需大量高保真仿真，因此亟需降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM首先在小子域上预训练神经算子，随后冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测结果，实现高效迁移。

Result: 在更大达西流域上，使用16次目标仿真后，误差比容量匹配模型低36-56%；在快速俯仰空气动力学问题中，20步场滚动预测误差显著降低，无论零样本还是少量样本校准均表现优异。

Conclusion: 局部预训练与组合级迁移的协同设计是构建物理基础模型的有前景原则。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [260] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一种用于设备端大语言模型推理的流式推测与优化框架，通过逐步细化计算、加载和缓存保留优先级，利用部分GPU结果实现细粒度的GPU执行与存储I/O重叠，显著降低内存使用并提升生成吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM推理面临模型权重远超可用DRAM的挑战，现有系统在准确稀疏执行决策与高效计算-I/O重叠之间存在根本性权衡，导致串行执行或冗余权重加载、额外计算及大缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream采用流式推测与优化机制，基于部分GPU结果逐步精炼计算、加载和缓存保留策略，实现细粒度的计算与I/O重叠。

Result: 相比先前的设备端LLM推理系统，LeanStream在最佳吞吐量下将内存使用减少4.8×至7.5×，同时将令牌生成吞吐量提升1.6×至2.1×。

Conclusion: LeanStream有效解决了设备端LLM推理中的内存与性能瓶颈，通过精细的推测-优化流水线实现了高效率与低内存占用的平衡。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [261] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了矩阵型连续思维模型中潜在矩阵Z的秩是否能反映并影响推理路径。通过多种读出机制和消融实验，发现无论是否引入非线性，秩-投影曲线均保持平坦，表明模型对秩不敏感；进一步对比显示，这种秩盲性可能源于位置无关性而非结构信息丢失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究矩阵潜变量中的秩是否作为推理路径的结构指标，验证其在任务性能中的作用。

Method: 采用矩阵-CODI模型，在四个训练设置下进行秩-k投影消融实验，并测试四种不同读出机制（双线性、双线性+GELU、SVD增强、二次型）下的表现；同时使用线性探测和负向控制（GPT-2 SFT）验证结果的稳健性。

Result: 所有读出方式下的秩-k曲线均平坦，误差范围小于0.6个百分点；线性探测表现差于预训练隐藏状态；负控实验也呈现类似现象，说明秩盲性可能是由位置无关性导致，而非结构信息缺失。

Conclusion: 矩阵潜变量中的秩不能有效追踪或影响多成分推理路径，模型对秩不敏感，其秩盲性更可能源于位置无关性而非结构信息丢失。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [262] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度光流的新型地球静止轨道大气运动矢量（AMV）提取方法，替代传统窗口交叉相关追踪，显著提升计算效率与精度。通过自监督几何残差损失与监督式探空仪重建进行微调，并将多卫星立体匹配的教师模型压缩为单卫星学生模型，实现全球全盘覆盖。学生模型模拟教师的卡方检验与高度不确定性以保障质量。验证表明，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9、7.3 μm）优于现有业务AMV，但在长波红外（11.2 μm）性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV依赖窗口交叉相关和NWP背景状态，存在循环依赖、计算成本高、覆盖率低等问题；立体风虽提高精度但计算复杂且需多卫星重叠。亟需更高效、高覆盖、无需多卫星协同的新方法。

Method: 采用深度光流替代窗口追踪，结合自监督几何残差损失与探空仪重建监督进行微调；通过知识蒸馏将多卫星立体匹配教师模型转化为单卫星学生模型，保留卡方统计与高度不确定性评估能力。

Result: 学生模型可在全盘地球静止卫星图像上生成全球风场；三重共现验证显示，立体风在6.2、6.9、7.3 μm水汽波段优于业务AMV，在11.2 μm长波红外波段性能下降。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了高效、高覆盖、高质量的单星大气运动矢量反演，显著提升了水汽波段风场精度，具备在数值天气预报中广泛应用的潜力。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [263] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在精算定价任务中是否存在类似于现代深度学习中的缩放定律，使用真实世界车险数据集，评估不同模型家族在增加训练数据时的表现。结果表明，所有模型均随数据增加而改进，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM表现出远强于纯监督的表格型Transformer和标准MLP基线的数据缩放能力；而Transformer变体除非引入额外归纳偏置（如TabM式适配或自监督），否则参数缩放效果弱。研究为基于数据规模的模型选择提供了量化指导，并强调架构与损失函数设计对有效缩放的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索精算定价任务中是否存在类似深度学习的缩放规律，以指导模型选择并理解不同架构在有限数据下的表现差异。

Method: 基于真实世界车险数据集，训练多种模型家族（包括TabM、表格型Transformer、MLP等）在不同数据比例下，通过多次随机种子实验，评估其在测试集上的泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）表现，分析数据量、模型容量与计算资源之间的缩放关系。

Result: 所有模型家族在更多数据下表现提升，但缩放指数差异大；TabM展现出最强的数据缩放能力，而标准Transformer变体在缺乏额外归纳偏置时缩放效果差。简单增大Transformer规模无法带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的模型缩放在精算表格式任务中高度依赖于架构设计与损失函数目标，仅增加模型规模不足以提升性能；建议根据数据规模选择合适模型，并引入如自监督或结构化归纳偏置来增强缩放能力。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [264] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种新算法，解决神经场在稀疏观测下高质量重建的难题。NTK-KIP通过学习精炼的支持集坐标实现高效非线性插值；MetaQuill通过元学习共享初始化，实现轻量级任务适应；MetaQuill-KIP融合二者，以非线性预热+小量微调实现高保真重建与语义合理补全，优于依赖大型生成先验的扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场在稀疏观测下难以实现快速高质量重建；经典NTK回归虽有闭式解但为线性且无法积累可复用的任务先验，限制了其在实际场景中的应用。

Method: 提出NTK-KIP（基于支持集的非线性插值）、MetaQuill（元学习共享初始化）和MetaQuill-KIP（结合两者优势），分别从非线性表示、可迁移特征学习和高效适应角度改进神经场建模。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下仍能实现高PSNR重建和语义合理的图像补全，仅需轻量级实例适应，显著优于依赖大规模预训练生成模型的扩散基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将NTK驱动的神经场扩展为非线性和可元学习的形式，本研究有效缩小了分析型核方法与实用少样本重建之间的差距，为高效、灵活的神经场建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult. Classical Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) regression gives closed-form fits, yet it is fundamentally linear and cannot accumulate reusable task priors. We develop three algorithms that address these gaps. NTK-KIP learns a distilled support set of coordinates (and optional labels) so that a finite NTK can inpaint large missing regions from little observed data, yielding a compact non-linear representation instead of a raw kernel solve. MetaQuill meta-learns a shared initialization for an INR so that new scenes can be adapted by updating only a small task-specific weight offset, which provides true feature learning and a reusable prior. Finally, MetaQuill-KIP fuses both ideas: it seeds the task with a KIP-style non-linear warm start, then refines only that small offset around the meta-learned initialization. MetaQuill-KIP achieves high-PSNR reconstructions and semantically plausible inpainting under very sparse observations, while requiring only lightweight per-instance adaptation, whereas diffusion-style baselines typically depend on large pretrained generative priors and costly per-image tuning. This shows that NTK-driven neural fields can be made both non-linear and meta-learnable, narrowing the gap between analytic kernels and practical few-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [265] [Routing Is Not Enough: Diagnosing Intra-Adapter Subspace Contention in MoE+LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03150)
*Mehreen Hossain Chowdhury,Nowshin Mahjabin,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,Md Azam Hossain,Abu Raihan Mostofa Kamal,Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多领域微调中，MoE路由与LoRA结合时，仅靠标记级路由是否足以实现领域特定更新。通过实验发现，尽管不同领域专家路由近乎不重叠，但加入生物医学数据仍显著增加代码困惑度，表明路由分离不足以防止负迁移。为此，作者提出Jaccard路由重叠和适配器梯度余弦相似性作为诊断工具，揭示问题源于几乎正交的领域梯度在相同低秩适配器子空间中的竞争。为解决此问题，提出SpawnLoRA，当检测到适配器层面冲突时动态在MoE专家内添加门控子适配器，同时保持路由器不变。在Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct和OLMoE-1B-7B上的评估显示，SpawnLoRA有效减少了负迁移，优于标准和秩自适应LoRA。结果表明，专家内部的结构分离可带来超越路由或秩扩展的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多领域微调中MoE+LoRA假设的合理性，即标记级路由能否真正实现领域隔离；发现现有方法存在负迁移问题，尤其在梯度方向差异大时，低秩适配器子空间中出现冲突。

Method: 引入Jaccard路由重叠与适配器梯度余弦相似性作为诊断指标，识别负迁移根源；提出SpawnLoRA，动态在MoE专家内生成门控子适配器以缓解适配器层级的梯度竞争。

Result: SpawnLoRA在多个模型和混合设置下显著降低负迁移，性能优于标准LoRA和秩自适应LoRA；证明专家内部结构设计对减少干扰至关重要。

Conclusion: 仅依赖路由分离或扩大秩无法完全避免负迁移；在专家内部引入动态结构（如子适配器）可有效提升多领域微调的稳定性与性能，表明结构设计在适配器机制中的关键作用。

Abstract: Multi-domain fine-tuning often combines MoE routing with LoRA, assuming that token-level routing separates domain-specific updates. We test this assumption in MoE+LoRA using Python code paired with biomedical text and mathematical reasoning. Although these domains show near-disjoint expert routing, adding biomedical data substantially increases code perplexity, indicating that routing separation alone may not prevent negative transfer. To localize the failure, we introduce Jaccard routing overlap and adapter-gradient cosine similarity, which measure expert sharing and update compatibility, respectively. These diagnostics indicate that interference arises mostly from nearly orthogonal domain gradients competing within the same low-rank adapter subspace. We address this issue with SpawnLoRA, which dynamically adds gated sub-adapters inside MoE experts when adapter-level contention is detected, while keeping the router fixed. We evaluate SpawnLoRA on Phi-tiny-MoE-instruct and OLMoE-1B-7B across multiple mixture settings and find that it effectively reduces negative transfer compared with standard and rank-adaptive LoRA. These results demonstrate that structural separation inside experts provides benefits beyond routing or rank expansion alone.

</details>


### [266] [Frontier LLMs are effective batch optimizers: Assessing reasoning models in continuous and discrete settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03177)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,David Graff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿大语言模型（LLMs）在批量优化任务中的表现，发现其在数值测试函数上具有竞争力但表现不稳定，而在语义丰富的离散空间中表现显著优于传统优化方法，表明其在与预训练数据结构相似的离散空间中具有更强的优化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索现代推理型大语言模型在批量优化场景下的有效性，特别是在连续和离散优化设置中的表现，以评估其作为优化先验的潜力。

Method: 通过零样本实验，评估当前前沿大语言模型在连续和离散优化任务中的表现，并对比经典非LLM优化方法。

Result: LLMs在数值测试函数上表现竞争但脆弱；在语义丰富、结构类似预训练数据的离散空间中表现显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 大语言模型作为批量优化器，在语义丰富的离散空间中表现出色，但在一般优化任务中仍不如传统方法稳定，其性能高度依赖于任务与预训练数据的匹配程度。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) have become attractive priors for optimization due to their large-scale pretraining that enables them to navigate a variety of optimization settings. However, the effectiveness of modern reasoning LLMs in batch optimization settings remains underexplored. Here we investigate the performance of the current generation of frontier LLMs as batch optimizers in both continuous and discrete settings. We find that while LLMs are competitive zero-shot batch optimizers for numerical test functions, their performance is brittle compared to classical non-LLM optimization approaches. However, LLM priors are significantly better in semantically rich settings, indicating that their batch optimization behavior is highly effective when navigating and reasoning over the discrete spaces most similar in structure to their pretraining data.

</details>


### [267] [Portable Causal Fairness Across Synthetic Data Generator Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03180)
*Steven Golob,Sikha Pentyala,Martine De Cock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将DECAF框架中的公平性定义从单一非私有GAN推广至九种不同生成器（基于边际、GAN和扩散模型，含差分隐私变体），在Adult和COMPAS数据集上进行了2520次配对实验。结果表明，该机制具有广泛可移植性，新提出的因果扩散模型在保持高数据保真度的同时实现了所有测试家族中最佳的公平性表现。添加隐私保护不会降低公平性，且对下游分类器性能影响极小（平均AUC损失仅0.07–0.15）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决合成数据发布中因生成器设计导致的不公平路径问题，确保在引入隐私保护的前提下仍能维持数据公平性。

Method: 将三种公平性定义转化为生成器因果图中的边剪切操作，并在多种生成器架构（边际、GAN、扩散）及不同隐私级别下验证其有效性。采用因果因子分解与差分隐私结合的方法实现公平性保障。

Result: 所提机制在所有测试生成器中均有效迁移；新提出的因果扩散模型在公平性上表现最优，同时保真度接近边际级水平；隐私保护未损害公平性，对下游任务影响微小。

Conclusion: 因果剪切机制具有强泛化能力，可在多种生成器与隐私设置下实现高效公平性保障，其中因果扩散模型为当前最优方案。

Abstract: When a statistical agency or regulator releases synthetic data in place of sensitive records, it chooses the generator that produces the table, and can shape that generator so unfair pathways are absent. DECAF made this concrete on one non-private GAN: three fairness definitions become three sets of edge cuts on the generator's causal graph. Whether the mechanism belongs to DECAF, or to causal factorisation itself, was untested. We port all three definitions to nine generators from three unrelated families (marginals-based, GAN, and diffusion, each with differentially private variants), across three levels of formal privacy guarantee, over 2,520 matched-pair runs on Adult and COMPAS datasets. The mechanism transfers everywhere, and our new causal diffusion backbone yields the fairest release of any family we tested, at fidelity close to the marginals tier. Applying the cut barely moves fidelity, only costs a downstream classifier about $0.07$ to $0.15$ AUC on average, and adding privacy guarantees don't make the data less fair.

</details>


### [268] [Language-encoded network topology enables large language models to reason about complex networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03229)
*Ucchwas Talukder Utsha,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Md Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioGlyph 是一种将网络拓扑转化为可解释且可迁移的结构角色语言的新方法，通过图划分和结构测量识别枢纽、社区核心和跨社区连接者等角色，并用固定规则转译为通用词汇。该表示法以结构角色、支持证据和语义后果描述每个元素，不改变网络或大语言模型本身。在20个跨越五个领域的网络中，BioGlyph 显著提升开放大语言模型回答结构推理问题的能力，相比基于边、数值或学习的表示方法，系统准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验表明，性能提升源于显式编码结构角色并以语义可解释形式表达。该优势在密集、具有社区结构的网络中更明显，在稀疏网络中减弱。在酵母蛋白互作网络中，揭示了跨社区连接者富集于必需基因，而外围蛋白则贫乏，体现了生物学组织规律。BioGlyph 为语言模型和科学家提供了一种可解释的网络结构推理工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理网络数据（如边列表、句子或表格）时，难以理解其深层结构含义，因为需要从文本中推断拓扑信息。现有方法缺乏对结构角色的显式、可解释表达，限制了模型在结构推理任务中的表现。因此，亟需一种能将网络拓扑转化为语义可解释、通用性强的结构角色语言的方法，以增强语言模型对网络结构的理解能力。

Method: BioGlyph 结合图划分算法与多种结构测量指标，自动识别节点在图中的结构性角色（如枢纽、社区核心、跨社区连接者），并依据预设规则将其映射为一套统一的语义词汇表。每个节点被表示为包含其结构角色、支持证据及潜在语义后果的描述性文本，保持原始网络和语言模型不变，仅在输入前进行结构化转换。

Result: 在20个来自生物、社会、电力、引文等领域的网络上测试，BioGlyph 显著提升开放大语言模型在结构推理任务上的表现，相较基线方法（边列表、数值向量、学习嵌入）准确率最高提升26个百分点。消融实验验证了结构角色显式编码的重要性；在密集且具社区结构的网络中效果更优，在稀疏网络中提升较小。在酵母蛋白互作网络中，发现跨社区连接者显著富集于必需基因，而外围节点则相反，体现生物学意义。

Conclusion: BioGlyph 成功将网络拓扑转化为一种可解释、可迁移的结构角色语言，显著增强了大语言模型对网络结构的理解与推理能力。该方法不仅提升模型性能，还揭示了真实世界网络中的生物学规律，为科学发现与人机协作提供了新范式。

Abstract: Networks describe systems in biology and beyond, from protein interactions and social relationships to power grids and citation records. Reasoning about such systems requires understanding their structure: which elements are central, which connections bridge separate communities, and how it changes when elements are removed. Although large language models (LLMs) excel at natural language, they struggle with such questions when networks are given as edge lists, sentences or measurement tables, because their structural meaning must be inferred. Here we introduce BioGlyph, which compiles network topology into an interpretable and transferable language of structural roles. BioGlyph combines graph partitioning and structural measurements to identify roles such as hubs, community cores and cross-community connectors, and fixed rules to translate them into a universal vocabulary. The representation describes each element through its structural role, supporting evidence and semantic consequences, leaving both the network and the LLM unchanged. Across twenty networks spanning five domains, BioGlyph substantially improves open LLMs' ability to answer structural reasoning questions, outperforming edge-based, numerical and learned representations by up to 26 percentage points in system accuracy. Ablations show that the gain comes from explicitly encoding structural roles in semantically interpretable terms. The gain is more prominent in dense, community-structured networks and diminishes in sparse networks whose topology is more readily inferred from text. In a budding-yeast protein-interaction network, BioGlyph exposes biological organization: cross-community connectors are enriched for essential genes, whereas peripheral proteins are depleted. BioGlyph thus provides an interpretable representation for both language models and scientists to reason about network structure.

</details>


### [269] [The 2026 PNPL Competition: Word Classification and Efficient Cross-Subject Generalisation in LibriBrain100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03231)
*Francesco Mantegna,Gereon Elvers,Dulhan Jayalath,Gilad Landau,Tasha Kim,Miran Özdogan,Luisa Kurth,Teyun Kwon,SungJun Cho,Benjamin Ballyk,Alex Fung,Anna Greer,Pratik Somaiya,Christian Herff,Yorguin Mantilla Ramos,Hamza Abdelhedi,Karim Jerbi,Greg Farquhar,Brendan Shillingford,Mark Woolrich,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 2026 PNPL竞赛基于LibriBrain100数据集，推进非侵入性语音解码的课程任务，分为深度（Deep）和广度（Broad）两个赛道：前者追求高精度的受试者内词分类性能，后者聚焦跨被试泛化能力，逐步减少个体微调数据量至10分钟，以实现临床可行的脑机接口应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非侵入性语音解码系统在大量个体数据上表现优异，但实际脑机接口需在极短时间内对新用户实现有效解码，因此需要提升模型跨被试泛化能力，推动向临床实用化迈进。

Method: 通过扩展LibriBrain数据集至100名被试（共约80小时个体数据），构建LibriBrain100；设立双赛道：深赛道（Deep）专注于最大化个体内词分类性能，广赛道（Broad）则评估模型在逐渐减少个体微调数据（40→20→10分钟）下的跨被试泛化能力。

Result: 2026年竞赛中，广赛道成功验证了在仅10分钟个体数据下实现有效跨被试词分类的可能性，为非侵入性脑机接口的临床应用提供了关键支持。

Conclusion: LibriBrain100数据集与双赛道设计有效推动了非侵入性语音解码从高精度个体建模向低资源、强泛化能力的临床应用演进，标志着迈向实用化脑机接口的重要一步。

Abstract: The ambition of the 2025 PNPL competition (Landau et al., 2025) was to launch a multi-year curriculum for non-invasive speech decoding. Designed to progress from foundational tasks toward the linguistic complexity required for a practical brain-computer interface (BCI), it set the stage with speech detection and phoneme classification tasks. Winning submissions reached F1-macro scores of 95.6% and 73.6% on the respective tasks (Elvers et al., 2026), highly significant advances. This success was built on the LibriBrain dataset (Özdogan et al., 2025), the largest within-subject MEG dataset recorded at the time with ${\sim}50$ hours of data for one subject. However, while within-subject scale drives strong decoding performance, a practical BCI must generalise to new users from minutes of data, not hours.
  The 2026 PNPL competition responds to this challenge with LibriBrain100 (Mantegna et al., 2026), an extended LibriBrain dataset with 32 additional subjects (${\sim}40$ minutes each) plus even more within-subject data (${\sim}80$ hours). Advancing the curriculum of tasks to focus on word classification, two complementary tracks are presented in this competition: the Deep track targets within-subject word classification at scale, aiming at the best possible performance; the Broad track targets cross-subject generalisation, progressively reducing the amount of subject-specific fine-tuning data from ${\sim}40$ to ${\sim}20$ to ${\sim}10$ minutes, a duration that falls within a clinically feasible range and brings us a step closer to a non-invasive BCI capable of restoring communication to people living with profound paralysis.

</details>


### [270] [B2B Customer Conversion Prediction: A Document Representation, Graph Theory, and CatBoost Driven Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03239)
*Tianqi Wang,Sheikh Shams Azam,Wan Eih Huang,Anton Wiranata,Christopher G. Brinton,Jan P. Allebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究B2B场景下长期销售周期中的客户转化预测问题，提出一种基于多键聚合客户数据的算法，并针对非标准化公司名称设计了处理拼写错误和变体的聚类架构。通过生成特征并使用CatBoost模型进行预测，框架达到91%的准确率，进而提出个性化营销建议以促进转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在B2B一次性销售场景中，购买周期可能长达数月甚至数年，因此识别高潜力客户并推荐个性化营销活动对有效营销至关重要。现有方法在客户数据聚合、特征生成及转化预测方面存在不足，亟需系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出多键客户数据聚合方法；针对非标准化公司名称设计新型聚类架构以处理拼写错误与变体；定义并生成一系列特征；采用CatBoost模型进行客户转化预测。

Result: 所提出的框架在客户转化预测任务上实现了91%的准确率，验证了其有效性。基于预测结果与模型分析，进一步提出了个性化营销活动推荐策略。

Conclusion: 该框架有效提升了B2B客户转化预测的准确性，为长期销售周期中的精准营销提供了可行的技术支持，具有较强的实践应用价值。

Abstract: In the one-time selling B2B context, the buying cycle may last months or even years. During the long process, targeting customers that have a high potential to make purchases and recommending personalized campaigns accordingly are important for effective marketing. For this goal, we study the following problems, B2B customer data aggregation, customer feature generation, and prediction of whether a B2B customer would show interest in making a purchase (i.e., prediction of conversion into sales funnel). We propose an algorithm to aggregate individual contacts to the B2B customer level based on multiple keys. For non-standardized keys such as company names, we propose a novel architecture to cluster them in a domain encompassing irregularities such as spelling mistakes and spelling variants. We then define and generate a set of features and apply the CatBoost model for customer conversion prediction. Our framework achieves 91\% prediction accuracy. Based on the prediction results and analysis of the model, we then discuss personalized campaign recommendations to foster conversion.

</details>


### [271] [FlowBalance: Verifier-Grounded Self-Improvement from On-Policy Reasoning Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03241)
*Zixun Huang,Kishan Panaganti,Haitao Mi,Leowei Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowBalance是一种基于验证器的自提升方法，通过轨迹平衡实现校准的自我指导，无需额外的逐标记模仿损失。它利用冻结的训练时策略生成分词级概率增益，并结合验证器提供的组优势对引导信号进行校准：在正优势轨迹上保留引导，在负优势轨迹上反转引导，无偏好时则禁用引导。该方法通过能量函数重新加权参考策略，以单个日志划分估计完成归一化目标分布，从而在数学推理任务中优于FlowRL，提升性能、训练速度与稳定性，并避免响应长度坍缩，同时提高正确策略多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自提升方法在使用自身策略经验时存在脆弱性：终端验证器提供可靠但稀疏的监督，而密集的同模型引导可能增强错误信心或使学习集中在狭窄解空间。需要一种能有效利用验证反馈并防止过拟合的方法。

Method: FlowBalance引入一个冻结的训练时间策略来生成每个轨迹的分词级概率增益，聚合为轨迹级自引导分数；再通过验证器提供的组优势对引导进行校准（保留、反转或禁用）；最后使用轨迹平衡技术，通过一次日志划分估计构建归一化目标分布，实现校准的自我指导。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，FlowBalance在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上均优于FlowRL，提升了平均表现，加快了训练速度并增强了稳定性；避免了OPS-D的响应长度坍缩问题，且在控制的AIME24诊断中表现出更高的正确策略多样性。

Conclusion: FlowBalance通过验证器校准的轨迹平衡机制，实现了高效、稳定且多样化的自提升，无需额外的模仿损失，是当前自提升方法的重要改进。

Abstract: A reasoning model can improve from its own on-policy experience, but this inner loop is fragile: terminal verifiers provide reliable yet sparse supervision, while dense same-model guidance can reinforce false confidence or overconcentrate learning on a narrow solution mode. We introduce FlowBalance, a verifier-grounded self-improvement method that learns a normalized distribution over complete responses. For each on-policy trajectory, a frozen training-time view of the same policy uses privileged context to produce token-level log-probability gains, which are aggregated into a trajectory-level self-guidance score. FlowBalance calibrates this score with the verifier-derived group advantage: guidance is retained on positive-advantage trajectories, reversed on negative-advantage trajectories, and disabled when the rollout group provides no outcome preference. The resulting energy exponentially reweights a reference policy, and profiled trajectory balance fits the normalized target with one log-partition estimate per rollout group.
